pressure in economic growth. Faced with a complicated and                                  觀 政 策， 企 業 改 善 管 理 和 生 產 模 式， 使 國 民
cha nging e x t e r n a l e n v i ro n m e n t, th e Ch i n e s e gov ernment              生產總值同比增長了 5%，讓經濟發展穩中有
has optimized a series of macroeconomic policies and                                       進。儘管如此，國內經濟的結構性調整使消費
enterprises have improved their management and production                                  者減少支出，並導致有效需求不足，也讓企業
models, which has resulted in a 5% year-on-year increase                                   在擴大生產和新增投資上都更加審慎保守。總

of growth. Nevertheless, adjustments to domestic economic                                  確定性依然很大，企業的經營壓力並未減少，
structure have resulted in less consumer spending and                                      降本增效和管控風險成為企業應對挑戰的策
hence an inadequate effective demand, and also made                                        略。
the enterprises to take a more cautious and conservative
manner in production expansion and new investments. In
general, considerable uncertainty remains when it comes to
the recovery of domestic and overseas economy and market

During the Reporting Period, efforts have been made by the                                 報告期內，集團管理層從市場、生產、風險管

and cooperations with clients from countries and regions in                                使用及生產效率，降低生產和營運成本；風險
Southeast Asia for market expansion and sales promotion.                                   管理方面，集團對新增投資和收購更為謹慎，
I n t e r m s o f p r o d u c t i o n, t h e G r o u p h a s i m p l e m e n t e d t h e   並制定出全面的 ESG 戰略及工作時間表，降低
“Lean Production” program in more plants to improve the                                    氣候風險對集團業務和財務的影響，增強業務
resource use and production efficiency and reduce production                               抗擊風險能力，強化集團可持續發展水平。
and operating costs. As for risk management, the Group
has exercised a more prudential attitude towards additional
investments and acquisitions and formulated a comprehensive
ESG strategy and work schedule, with an aim to mitigate the
impact of climate risks on the business and finance of the

strengthen the sustainable development of the Group.

     concurs with the views of the People’s Bank of China on                                          穩定。
     the RMB exchange rate, that is, the RMB exchange rate has

RECONCILIATION OF HKFRS MEASURES TO THE                                                         香港財務報告準則計量與非香港財務報
NON-HKFRS MEASURES                                                                              告準則計量之對賬
For review of financial performance, the Group has provided non-                                就 財 務 表 現 回 顧， 集 團 已 提 供 非 香 港 財 務 報
HKFRS measures, including adjusted EBITDA, adjusted EBITDA                                      告 準 則 計 量， 包 括 經 調 整 EBITDA、 經 調 整
margin, adjusted operating profit and adjusted EBIT margin,                                     EBITDA 率、 經 調 整 營 運 盈 利 及 經 調 整 EBIT
which are supplementary to the Group’s consolidated results in                                  率，作為集團根據香港財務報告準則呈列的綜
accordance with HKFRS. The Group believes that these additional                                 合業績之補充。集團相信，上述額外數據能為
figures provide our shareholders and investors with useful                                      股東及投資者提供有用補充資料，透過剔除部
supplementary information to facilitate the analysis and assessment                             分非現金項目（包括於簡明綜合收益表確認之
of the performance of the Group’s core operations by excluding                                  股份為基礎的薪酬開支、商譽減值以及機器、
certain non-cash items, which consist of share-based compensation                               設備及無形資產減值）有助於分析及評估集團
expenses, impairment of goodwill, and impairment of plant,                                      核心經營表現。該等非香港財務報告準則計量
equipment and intangible assets, recognised in the condensed                                    亦允許集團評估其持續經營，並用於內部規劃
consolidated income statement. These non-HKFRS measures also                                    及預測。
allow the Group to evaluate its ongoing operations and are applied
for internal planning and forecasting purposes.

The use of these non-HKFRS measures may have certain limitations                                採用該等非香港財務報告準則計量作為分析及
as a tool for analysis and comparison. Shareholders and investors                               比較工具或存在一定的局限性。故建議股東及
are advised not to consider these non-HKFRS measures in isolation                               投資者不應將其與集團根據香港財務報告準則
from, or as a substitute for analysis of, the Group’s financial                                 所呈報的財務表現分開考慮或視作替代分析。
performance as reported under HKFRS. Also, please note that these                               此外，該等非香港財務報告準則計量的定義可
non-HKFRS measures may be defined differently from similar terms                                能有別於其他公司使用的類似詞彙。
used by other companies.

     (5)     According to Article 5 of the Regulatory Guidelines for Listed                                (5)   根據《上市公司監管指引第 2 號－上市公司募集

      is Clarendon House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11,                2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11,
      Bermuda.                                                            Bermuda。

      Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.                                      限公司上市。

      Company and its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”)             屬 公 司（「本 集 團 」）之 業 務 主 要 在 中 華
      are mainly engaged in the research and development                  人 民 共 和 國（「中 國 」）研 究 及 發 展（「研
      (“R&D”), production, distribution and sale of flavours              發 」）、 生 產、 分 銷 及 銷 售 香 精、 食 品 配
      and fragrances, and food ingredients (“F&F and Food                 料（「香 精 及 食 品 配 料 」）、 煙 用 原 料、 香
      ingredients”), tobacco raw materials, aroma raw                     原 料 及 調 味 品 產 品。 朱 林 瑤 女 士（「朱 女
      materials and condiment products in the People’s                    士」）乃本公司之董事會主席，於 2024 年
      Republic of China (the “PRC”). Ms. Chu Lam Yiu (“Ms.                6 月 30 日，朱女士持有本公司約 71% 之

      holds approximately 71% beneficial equity interest in the

                  The Company’s management reviews the provision                                                        本公司管理層持續審查商譽的減值
                  for goodwill impairment on an ongoing basis,                                                          撥 備 並 通 常 按 年 審 查， 如 若 出 現
                  generally annually and more frequently if there are                                                   任何可能導致潛在減值的事件或
                  any events or changes in circumstances that would                                                     情 況 變 化， 則 審 查 頻 率 會 相 應 提
                  give rise to potential impairment. The Company                                                        高。 本 公 司 採 用 收 益 法（亦 即 貼 現
                  uses the income approach (i.e. discounted cash                                                        現 金 流 量 模 型 ）作 為 有 關 計 提 的 評
                  flow models) as the evaluation method for such                                                        估方法。採用這種方法的原因乃根
                  provision. The reason for adopting this method is                                                     據《香 港 會 計 準 則 第 36 號 － 資 產 減
                  that according to HKAS 36 — Impairment of Assets,                                                     值 》， 商 譽 減 值 評 估 通 常 採 用 收 益
                  goodwill impairment assessment usually uses the                                                       法來確定 CGU 之價值。具有足夠長
                  income approach to determine the value of a CGU.                                                      時間之財務歷史的 CGU 則相應具備
                  CGU that have a sufficiently long financial history                                                   預測未來業績的基礎，在這種情況
                  would have a basis for predicting future results,                                                     下，CGU 之商譽的可收回金額將基
                  and in such case, the recoverable amount of                                                           於 使 用 價 值（「VIU」）而 定。 由 於 集
                  goodwill of the CGU would be based on the value                                                       團 各 個 CGUs 均 為 具 有 較 長 時 間 之
                  in use (“VIU”). As each of the Group’s CGUs is CGU                                                    財務歷史的 CGU、具備預測其未來
                  having a long financial history which could provide                                                   業績的基礎，故滿足使用貼現現金
                  the basis for predicting its future results, it satisfied                                             流量模型的條件，因此可以根據歷
                  the criteria for the application of the discounted                                                    史數據進行其收益的預測。
                  cash flow models and hence its profitability forecast
